- **bizdev**: Business Development lead. Owns partner tiering, joint go-to-market, partner enablement, channel-program design, strategic-relationship management. Use for partner-program work, channel-strategy decisions, joint-GTM planning, partner-enablement asset review. ([realgrapedrop/grapedrop-playbook](https://github.com/realgrapedrop/grapedrop-playbook/blob/HEAD/reference/agents/bizdev.md) · ★2)
- **bizdev-lead**: The lead of the bizdev team (sales and business development): a very experienced generalist on the fractional-executive model. Consult through the equerry-team skill for any ask about pipeline, prospects, proposals, statements of work, rate cards, offers, consulting or training sales, new clients, partnerships. Returns a coverage verdict, then the work or a brief for a professional, then any proposed primitive as text. Never edits files. ([thorwhalen/equerry](https://github.com/thorwhalen/equerry/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/bizdev-lead.md))
- **bizdev-outbound-strategist**: Use when planning outbound B2B outreach, cold-email campaigns, or relationship-building with partners/brokers/associations for one company (find a co-founder is partner-recruiter's lane). Drafts, lists, plans only; never sends. Not for inbound customer leads (use revenue-ops-architect). ([1chrisshannon1-afk/claude-agentic-runtime-public](https://github.com/1chrisshannon1-afk/claude-agentic-runtime-public/blob/HEAD/live/agents/bizdev-outbound-strategist.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **bizdev-outbound-strategist**: `bizdev-outbound-strategist` is the venture department's outbound and relationship-building specialist (`agents/bizdev-outbound-strategist.md` frontmatter's `description:` key). ([1chrisshannon1-afk/claude-agentic-runtime-public](https://github.com/1chrisshannon1-afk/claude-agentic-runtime-public/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/bizdev-outbound-strategist.md) · ★1)
- **bizdev_lead**: 사업개발(가설검증·파이프라인) 리드 정의 ([Ratenne/price-pulse](https://github.com/Ratenne/price-pulse/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/bizdev_lead.md))
- **bizlogic-hunter**: Delegates to this agent when the user wants to test for business logic flaws, find workflow bypass vulnerabilities, detect price manipulation or payment tampering, identify race conditions in transactions, test authorization boundaries between user roles, or discover logic errors that standard vulnerability scanners miss during authorized penetration testing. ([0xSteph/pentest-ai-agents](https://github.com/0xSteph/pentest-ai-agents/blob/HEAD/agents/bizlogic-hunter.md) · ★2.5k · 7 copies)
- **bizlogic-hunter**: Delegates to this agent when the user wants to test for business logic flaws, find workflow bypass vulnerabilities, detect price manipulation or payment tampering, identify race conditions in transactions, test authorization boundaries between user roles, or discover logic errors that standard vulnerability scanners miss during all penetration testing. ([Iamatto01/SentinelAI](https://github.com/Iamatto01/SentinelAI/blob/HEAD/agents/bizlogic-hunter.md) · ★2)
- **bizops**: Business operations specialist covering market analysis, pricing strategy, go-to-market planning, and competitive intelligence. Use proactively when evaluating business viability, analyzing markets, or planning launches. ([pcatattacks/solopreneur-plugin](https://github.com/pcatattacks/solopreneur-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/bizops.md) · ★16)
- **bizplan**: 사업계획서 에이전트 — 정부지원사업(예비창업·초기창업패키지 등) PSST 양식 사업계획서 섹션 작성과 평가위원 관점 리뷰. 사업계획서 작성·첨삭 요청에 사용. ([kimlawtech/speciai-plugin](https://github.com/kimlawtech/speciai-plugin/blob/HEAD/speciai-advisory/agents/bizplan.md))
- **bizplan-costs**: You are the **Startup Costs & Ongoing Expenses Subagent**, one of 5 parallel subagents launched during `/bizplan analyze &lt;business-type&gt;`. Your specific responsibility is evaluating **Capital Requirements**, which accounts for **25% of the overall Business Viability Score**. ([svetlasyrimis/ai-business-plan-claude](https://github.com/svetlasyrimis/ai-business-plan-claude/blob/HEAD/agents/bizplan-costs.md))
- **bizplan-financial**: Specialist on the team-business-plan pipeline. Builds the revenue model, pricing, cost structure, and rough unit economics from the team-research dossier's monetization findings and the viability run's market material — does not re-research monetization from scratch. Runs in parallel with the offering and gtm specialists, after triage. ([saralafleur/ember-foundation-substrate-toolkit](https://github.com/saralafleur/ember-foundation-substrate-toolkit/blob/HEAD/plugins/ember-foundation-substrate-toolkit/agents/bizplan-financial.md))
- **bizplan-financial**: You are the **Financial Projections Subagent**, one of 5 parallel subagents launched during `/bizplan analyze &lt;business-type&gt;`. Your specific responsibility is evaluating **Financial Viability**, which accounts for **25% of the overall Business Viability Score**. ([svetlasyrimis/ai-business-plan-claude](https://github.com/svetlasyrimis/ai-business-plan-claude/blob/HEAD/agents/bizplan-financial.md))
- **bizplan-gtm**: Specialist on the team-business-plan pipeline. Defines target segments, competitive positioning, and channel strategy at business-plan altitude — deliberately high-level, leaving detailed go-to-market execution to the Marketing Division's future team-gtm. Runs in parallel with the offering and financial specialists, after triage. ([saralafleur/ember-foundation-substrate-toolkit](https://github.com/saralafleur/ember-foundation-substrate-toolkit/blob/HEAD/plugins/ember-foundation-substrate-toolkit/agents/bizplan-gtm.md))
- **bizplan-judge**: Adversarially challenges the combined plan from the offering, financial, and gtm specialists on the team-business-plan pipeline — investor-skeptic lens. Runs after all three specialists finish, before the lead. Read-only; not a rubber stamp. ([saralafleur/ember-foundation-substrate-toolkit](https://github.com/saralafleur/ember-foundation-substrate-toolkit/blob/HEAD/plugins/ember-foundation-substrate-toolkit/agents/bizplan-judge.md))
- **bizplan-lead**: Lead / synthesizer for the team-business-plan pipeline. Reconciles the offering, financial, and gtm specialists' drafts with the judge's challenge into one coherent business-plan.md. Runs last, after the judge. Owns ~/.claude/skills/team-business-plan/memory/run-log.md. ([saralafleur/ember-foundation-substrate-toolkit](https://github.com/saralafleur/ember-foundation-substrate-toolkit/blob/HEAD/plugins/ember-foundation-substrate-toolkit/agents/bizplan-lead.md))
- **bizplan-market**: You are the **Market Research & Competition Subagent**, one of 5 parallel subagents launched during `/bizplan analyze &lt;business-type&gt;`. Your specific responsibility is evaluating **Market Opportunity**, which accounts for **15% of the overall Business Viability Score**. ([svetlasyrimis/ai-business-plan-claude](https://github.com/svetlasyrimis/ai-business-plan-claude/blob/HEAD/agents/bizplan-market.md))
- **bizplan-offering**: Specialist on the team-business-plan pipeline. Defines the offering — MVP scope, product roadmap, and differentiation — grounded in the viability run's real-artifact product assessment, not a fresh guess. Runs in parallel with the financial and gtm specialists, after triage. ([saralafleur/ember-foundation-substrate-toolkit](https://github.com/saralafleur/ember-foundation-substrate-toolkit/blob/HEAD/plugins/ember-foundation-substrate-toolkit/agents/bizplan-offering.md))
- **bizplan-requirements**: You are the **Legal & Regulatory Requirements Subagent**, one of 5 parallel subagents launched during `/bizplan analyze &lt;business-type&gt;`. Your specific responsibility is evaluating **Regulatory Readiness**, which accounts for **20% of the overall Business Viability Score**. ([svetlasyrimis/ai-business-plan-claude](https://github.com/svetlasyrimis/ai-business-plan-claude/blob/HEAD/agents/bizplan-requirements.md))
- **bizplan-staffing**: You are the **Staffing & Operations Subagent**, one of 5 parallel subagents launched during `/bizplan analyze &lt;business-type&gt;`. Your specific responsibility is evaluating **Operational Feasibility**, which accounts for **15% of the overall Business Viability Score**. ([svetlasyrimis/ai-business-plan-claude](https://github.com/svetlasyrimis/ai-business-plan-claude/blob/HEAD/agents/bizplan-staffing.md))
- **bizplan-triage**: Intake clerk for the team-business-plan pipeline. Ingests a completed team-viability run (verdict.md + supporting files), enforces the verdict gate (no verdict → stop; DON'T BUILD → requires the user's explicit override; NEEDS MORE EVIDENCE → requires the user's explicit go-ahead), and normalizes everything into a planning brief. First agent in the pipeline. ([saralafleur/ember-foundation-substrate-toolkit](https://github.com/saralafleur/ember-foundation-substrate-toolkit/blob/HEAD/plugins/ember-foundation-substrate-toolkit/agents/bizplan-triage.md))

- **bl-builder**: Builds ONE branch of an approved build design, in its own git worktree, requirement by requirement. Station ③ of the buildloop. Works only from the written doc — never from conversation, never from its own judgment about what the doc "meant". Never proves its own work. ([zanybu/plot-and-prove](https://github.com/zanybu/plot-and-prove/blob/HEAD/agents/bl-builder.md))
- **bl-fixer**: Fix con causa ignota, invariante, dato di produzione, o che tocca due repo insieme. Apre la PR. ([valerielinc-ops/frontaliere-workspace](https://github.com/valerielinc-ops/frontaliere-workspace/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/bl-fixer.md))
- **bl-fixer-lite**: Applica una fix meccanica gia' pianificata, max 3 file, causa nota. Apre la PR. ([valerielinc-ops/frontaliere-workspace](https://github.com/valerielinc-ops/frontaliere-workspace/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/bl-fixer-lite.md))
- **bl-gate**: Reviews a build design against this repo's own laws — its rubric documents, architecture, ownership and the doctrine — BEFORE any code is written. Asks what this writes, and above all what READS it back. Returns APPROVED or a named rejection with the constraints the rewrite must satisfy. Station ②ᵇ of the buildloop. Never rewrites the doc, never edits code. ([zanybu/plot-and-prove](https://github.com/zanybu/plot-and-prove/blob/HEAD/agents/bl-gate.md))
- **bl-init-expert**: Git-branchless initialization specialist for setting up repos with branchless workflow ([jpoutrin/product-forge](https://github.com/jpoutrin/product-forge/blob/HEAD/plugins/git-workflow/agents/bl-init-expert.md) · ★16)
- **bl-integrator**: Owns the SEAMS between branches of a build — the defects no single branch owner can see. Merges branch worktrees, hunts contradictions between them, and runs the whole-build check no individual builder ran. Station ③ᶜ of the buildloop. Exists because "many authors, no integrator" is the root cause behind an entire class of worst-bug. ([zanybu/plot-and-prove](https://github.com/zanybu/plot-and-prove/blob/HEAD/agents/bl-integrator.md))
- **bl-planner**: Legge una issue (o un cluster) e il codice, e produce la scheda a 5 campi da far ratificare all'orchestratore. Non implementa. ([valerielinc-ops/frontaliere-workspace](https://github.com/valerielinc-ops/frontaliere-workspace/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/bl-planner.md))
- **bl-planner**: Writes the build design through the lens of the profession that actually owns this class of problem. Works out what KIND of problem it is, who does it for a living, how those practitioners approach it, and drafts the full build doc in their idiom using the /buildplan format. Station ② of the buildloop — runs after the audit squad, hands its doc to bl-gate. Writes only the build doc; never touches source. ([zanybu/plot-and-prove](https://github.com/zanybu/plot-and-prove/blob/HEAD/agents/bl-planner.md))
- **bl-prover**: Runs an approved build doc's acceptance checks against what was actually built — from a context that never saw the build session's reasoning. Classifies every result HARNESS-PROVEN or LIVE-PROVEN, hunts the four ways a harness lies, and returns a per-check verdict. Station ③ᵇ of the buildloop. Never fixes what it finds, never edits source. ([zanybu/plot-and-prove](https://github.com/zanybu/plot-and-prove/blob/HEAD/agents/bl-prover.md))
- **bl-rootcause**: Takes a list of symptoms and collapses them into the smallest set of real defects. Exists because four separate red findings once turned out to be ONE bug, and because a retrieval problem got four wrong diagnoses before anyone found the corrupt index underneath all of them. Station ① of the buildloop, after bl-scout and bl-runtime report. Never edits. ([zanybu/plot-and-prove](https://github.com/zanybu/plot-and-prove/blob/HEAD/agents/bl-rootcause.md))
- **bl-runtime**: Read-only reality check on what is ACTUALLY running. Compares the config file against the RUNNING process environment, checks service state, deployed version drift, and data-store stats. Exists because config in a file is not config in a process. Station ① of the buildloop. Never restarts, never writes. ([zanybu/plot-and-prove](https://github.com/zanybu/plot-and-prove/blob/HEAD/agents/bl-runtime.md))
- **bl-scout**: Rimisura la claim di una issue del backlog frontaliereticino. Conta, legge un artifact, verifica se il difetto esiste ancora. Non modifica nulla. ([valerielinc-ops/frontaliere-workspace](https://github.com/valerielinc-ops/frontaliere-workspace/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/bl-scout.md))
- **bl-scout**: Read-only mapper for a codebase. Answers "where does X live", "what actually runs on the live path", "what's already been tried" — without polluting the main session's context. - **BLE_THRESHOLD_TEST_README**: 1. **Test Toggle**: `BLE_THRESHOLD_TEST_ENABLED = true` (set to false to disable) 2. **Fast Heartbeat**: Sends padded pings every 3 seconds instead of 30 seconds 3. **Large Packets**: Each ping includes 240 bytes of padding to match RTMP packet size 4. **Byte Tracking**: Accurately counts total bytes sent including protocol overhead 5. **Failure Detection**: Monitors for missed pong responses ([Mentra-Community/MentraOS](https://github.com/Mentra-Community/MentraOS/blob/HEAD/agents/BLE_THRESHOLD_TEST_README.md) · ★2.4k)

- **blindqa-verifier**: Verifies ONE blindqa finding — looks at its screenshot and the code behind it and returns a verdict (real bug / environment-only / robot mistake) with evidence. Use only after the user agreed to use a sub-agent, one finding per call, with the finding text, screenshot path, route and likely source file given in the prompt. ([DevZonayed/blindqa](https://github.com/DevZonayed/blindqa/blob/HEAD/agents/blindqa-verifier.md))
- **blindspot**: Peripheral vision audit for empirical output. Finds what the author cannot see — problems hiding in plain sight (vices) and opportunities being overlooked (virtues). Use when output exists and interpretation is about to happen. Inspired by Viktor Shklovsky's defamiliarization and a Jason Fletcher observation on Scott Cunningham's Substack. Read-only with respect to project files; writes its own report at `reviews/&lt;scope&gt;/blindspot/&lt;YYYY-MM-DD-HHMM&gt;.md` (where &lt;scope&gt; is the paper slug or _project). Launched as a fresh-context agent because by definition the producing context cannot see its ow… ([flonat/flonat-research](https://github.com/flonat/flonat-research/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/blindspot.md) · ★153 · 2 copies)
- **blindspot-researcher**: You find what nobody named. The ticket's own claims belong to another shard — **do not verify them**, and do not let them steer you. Anything the ticket already points at is, by definition, not your job.
